Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in algebraic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 251 and the invariant I(A,B) = 412 mod 4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to algebraic structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 251/412 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the algebraic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 171 and the convergence rate is O(792/n^1).
